5977	He packed each toy separately in its own little box.	one at a time	Separate plus ly.
5978	Her voice sounded strained as she spoke.	not natural or relaxed	Strain plus ed.
5979	We will revamp the old room with fresh paint.	to fix up and make new	Re plus vamp: new look.
5981	It was a coincidence that we wore the same shirt.	two things happening by chance together	Coin plus incidence: coins land alike.
5982	Calm blue water filled the lagoon near the sea.	a shallow pool of water by the sea	Lagoon rhymes with moon.
5983	The enthusiastic crowd at the game cheered very loudly.	very excited and eager	Enthusiasm is energy.
5984	The incidence of colds goes up in winter.	how often something happens	Incident plus ce.
5985	The old bridge can withstand strong winds.	to hold up against	With plus stand: stand with strength.
5986	He will groan when he sees the homework.	to make a low sound of unhappiness	Groan sounds like a low moan.
5987	Wait a min and I will be ready.	a unit of sixty seconds	Min is short for minute.
5988	The sampling of apples showed they were ripe.	choosing a small piece to test	Sample plus ing.
5989	It was evidently raining outside, since her coat was wet.	in a way that is easy to see	Evident plus ly.
5991	The cake was specially made for her.	in a special way	Special plus ly.
5992	He will grumble when he has to clean his room.	to complain in a low voice	Grumble sounds like a grumpy rumble.
5993	The landlord fixed the leaky pipe.	a person who owns a home that others rent	Land plus lord.
5994	The baby has a dependency on her parents.	needing help from someone	Depend plus ency.
5995	The shark swam near the boat.	a large fish with sharp teeth	Shark has a sharp fin.
5996	The mall was a hive of busy shoppers.	a busy place full of people	Hive buzzes with bees.
5997	My little sister is sixteen years old this fall.	one more than fifteen	Six plus teen.
5998	A spoonful of cornstarch will thicken the soup nicely.	to make thicker	Thick plus en.
5999	He will flatter her to get a cookie.	to praise too much	Flatter sounds like flat chatter.
